The NRD models advance roughly four orders of magnitude faster in simulated time than the high-fidelity simulator scenes they replace, making iterative on-policy learning practical and supporting neural reduced dynamics as a bridge between accurate but expensive physics simulation and scalable robot learning.

</details>


### [132] [SCAPE: Scenario-Conditioned Simulation-Augmented Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19425)
*Dijie Zhu,Seunghun Oh,Ruopeng Huang,Zhiyu Huang,Jiaqi Ma,Chen Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SCAPE框架，一种场景条件化的仿真增强策略评估框架，通过有限配对仿真-真实样本和大规模仿真回放，预测策略在真实世界场景条件下性能，并利用保形预测校准不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真增强的评估方法只关注总体平均性能，忽略了场景特定的性能变化，无法为策略安全部署提供具体指导。现实世界测试虽然真实但成本高、难扩展；纯仿真测试又存在sim-to-real差距。

Method: SCAPE框架包含两个关键组件：1) 在训练预测模型之前，先校正仿真标签中的sim-to-real偏差；2) 通过保形预测（conformal prediction）校准预测不确定性。利用少量配对仿真-真实数据和大量仿真回放来训练场景条件下的性能预测模型。

Result: 在自动驾驶和四足机器人速度跟踪两个任务上验证。Sim-to-sim研究中，相比场景条件神经基线和聚合统计基线，场景级预测错误分别降低4.9%/34.7%（驾驶）和14.5%/27.7%（四足）。在物理Unitree Go2上的实际部署也验证了有效性，同时提高了测试样本效率、产生更窄的校准预测区间、对分布外场景泛化更好。

Conclusion: SCAPE能够提供场景级别的细粒度性能预测，帮助制定更精细的部署策略，比传统的总体平均评估方法更能指导策略的安全部署。

Abstract: Reliable performance evaluation is a central bottleneck for deploying robot-learning policies in real-world conditions. Real-world testing is faithful but costly and difficult to scale, whereas simulation-based testing scales easily but is inevitably biased by the sim-to-real gap. Existing simulation-augmented methods combine limited real-world rollouts with abundant simulation proxies, but focus on performance averaged over initial conditions and deployment settings. Such population-level averages obscure scenario-specific variation and provide limited guidance about when and where a policy can be safely deployed. We propose SCAPE, a scenario-conditioned simulation-augmented policy evaluation framework that predicts scenario-conditioned real-world policy performance using limited paired sim-and-real samples and large-scale simulation rollouts. SCAPE corrects sim-to-real bias in simulation labels before training the prediction model and calibrates prediction uncertainty through conformal prediction. We validate SCAPE on autonomous driving and quadruped velocity tracking. In sim-to-sim studies, SCAPE reduces scenario-level prediction error by 4.9%/34.7% (driving) and 14.5%/27.7% (quadruped) relative to scene-conditioned neural and aggregate statistical baselines on average. We further evaluate a velocity-tracking policy deployed on a physical Unitree Go2. SCAPE also improves testing sample efficiency, produces narrower calibrated prediction intervals, generalizes better to out-of-distribution scenarios, and enables fine-grained deployment strategies.

</details>


### [133] [Hybrid Feedback Sampling for Sample-Efficient Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19443)
*Chaoyi Pan,Zeji Yi,John Zhang,Zachary Manchester,Guannan Qu,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈采样MPC（FS-MPC）算法，通过优化反馈策略实现最优采样提议分布，并采用混合采样设计在局部搜索与全局搜索间取得平衡，在理论分析与真实机器人实验中均验证了其对标准MPPI和纯反馈采样的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的MPC虽然因并行化和灵活性在机器人控制中广泛应用，但对高维、开环不稳定的动态系统，所需样本数随控制时域呈指数增长，导致采样效率低下和数值不稳定。因此需要解决采样策略在复杂动态系统中的效率和稳定性问题。

Method: FS-MPC通过优化反馈策略实现最优采样提议分布，并采用混合采样设计，根据系统稳定性和可用计算预算在局部搜索与全局搜索之间动态平衡。提供了理论分析证明混合采样相比标准MPPI收敛更快、相比标准反馈采样最优性更优。

Result: 在多样化的接触丰富控制任务（如人形机器人移动操作和灵巧操作）中，FS-MPC成功应对了标准采样方法难以处理的动态不稳定任务，并严格优于单独的反馈策略。最后在真实世界的人形机器人移动和操作任务中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: FS-MPC通过混合采样设计有效解决了采样型MPC在动态不稳定系统中的样本效率问题，理论与实验均表明其兼具快速收敛和良好最优性，为复杂机器人系统控制提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Thanks to its parallelizability and flexibility, sampling-based Model Predictive Control (MPC) has become widely popular for controlling real-world robotic systems. However, for high-dimensional and open-loop unstable dynamical systems, the required number of samples to improve the control sequence will grow exponentially with the horizon, leading to poor sample efficiency and numerical instability. This paper investigates the instability of shooting methods in sampling-based MPC and shows that the optimal sampling proposal distribution can be realized by sampling with an optimized feedback policy. We refer to this algorithm as Feedback Sampling MPC (FS-MPC). FS-MPC involves a hybrid sampling design which balances local and global search based on the system stability and the available computation budget. Our theoretical analysis shows that our hybrid sampling approach achieves faster convergence than standard MPPI and better optimality than standard feedback sampling. Empirically, in diverse contact-rich control tasks like humanoid loco-manipulation and dexterous manipulation, we show that FS-MPC successfully tackles dynamically unstable tasks where standard sample-based approaches struggle, and strictly outperforms feedback policies alone. Finally, we validate our method on humanoid robot locomotion and manipulation tasks in the real world.

</details>


### [134] [When Automata Meet Streams: Temporal Logic Compilation for Stream-Based Robotics Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19453)
*Sayem Nazmuz Zaman,Cyrus Neary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAM-TD方法，通过将任意LTL_f时序约束编译为自动机并将其回归的守卫嵌入到动作模式中，在不修改底层规划器的情况下，使基于流的机器人任务与运动规划（TAMP）能够满足时序约束，并在三个PDDLStream环境及标准PDDL基准上首次验证了流式TAMP在LTL_f约束下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于流的TAMP规划器只关注目标可达性，而长时程任务还需要满足时序规格（如安全关键的顺序、不变性和活性约束）。由于流式规划通过迭代细化动态生成不断扩展的几何对象集，导致现有的时序逻辑编译技术无法直接适用，目前没有方法能在流式求解器中强制执行此类时序约束。

Method: SAM-TD将任意LTL_f约束编译为自动机，并将回归后的自动机守卫嵌入到动作模式中（在规划开始前预先指定），从而处理规划期间由流动态生成的对象，无需枚举固定对象集或修改底层规划器。在搜索过程中，SAM-TD同步更新自动机状态，并使用跨所有自动机共享的有效性标记来剪枝违反约束的分支。

Result: 实验在三个机器人PDDLStream环境中首次实现了流式TAMP在LTL_f约束下的求解，并在标准离散PDDL基准上与最先进的时序约束编译方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: SAM-TD成功地将任意LTL_f时序约束编译方法扩展到基于流的TAMP规划器中，支持规划搜索期间由流迭代细化动态生成的流对象，填补了该领域的空白，为机器人长时程任务中的安全时序约束满足提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Stream-based robotics Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) integrates discrete symbolic planning with dynamically generated continuous geometric parameters, such as poses, grasps, and trajectories. However, stream-based planners typically reason only about goal reachability, whereas long-horizon tasks also demand adherence to temporal specifications, such as safety-critical ordering, invariance, and liveness constraints. No methods currently exist to enforce such temporal constraints for stream-based solvers because streams generate an expanding geometric object set via iterative stream refinement loops during planning, rendering existing temporal-logic compilation techniques incompatible. We therefore present Synchronous Action Monitoring with Token Destruction (SAM-TD), a compilation method that enforces arbitrary Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces ($\textrm{LTL}_f$) specifications in stream-based TAMP. SAM-TD translates arbitrary $\textrm{LTL}_f$ constraints into automata and embeds regressed automaton guards into action schemas, which are pre-specified before planning begins. By doing so, SAM-TD can handle objects generated by streams during planning, thus circumventing the need to enumerate a fixed object set or modify the underlying planner. During search, SAM-TD synchronously updates automaton states and uses a validity token shared across all automata to prune constraint-violating branches. We show that SAM-TD supports dynamically generated stream objects from iterative stream refinements during plan search. Experimental results provide the first ever demonstration of stream-based TAMP under $\textrm{LTL}_f$ constraints in three robotics PDDLStream environments. Furthermore, on standard discrete PDDL benchmarks, SAM-TD is competitive with state-of-the-art temporal-constraint compilation methods.

</details>


### [135] [Fine-Tuning VLAs with Self-Demonstrated Generative Control for Multi-Task Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19490)
*Prachi Garg,Steve Xing,Prahit Yaugand,Saurabh Gupta,Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自监督方法，利用零样本VLA模型生成的在线交互rollout作为额外训练数据来微调VLA模型，从而在新机器人上同时保留原有指令跟随能力和行为先验，同时学习新的专家技能，并提高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最先进的VLA模型在新机器人部署时，即使硬件配置存在轻微差异也会导致性能严重下降。现有的微调方法虽然能提升专家任务表现，但会损失原有的指令跟随能力和行为先验。

Method: 提出一种自监督方案：将零样本VLA模型生成的在线交互rollout作为微调的额外训练数据，与专家数据联合训练，使模型在不同任务间取得平衡。

Result: 在真实ALOHA机器人和RoboTwin新模拟基准上的实验表明，该方法能得到强多任务策略：(1)继承零样本模型蒸馏的先前任务；(2)保持通用指令跟随能力；(3)从专家数据学习新技能且样本效率更高。

Conclusion: 该自监督微调方案有效解决了VLA跨机器人部署中微调导致的灾难性知识遗忘问题，实现了知识保留与新技能学习之间的良好平衡。

Abstract: State-of-the-art vision-language-action (VLA) models such as $π_{0.5}$ exhibit strong semantic understanding, instruction following and task behavior. However, when deployed on new robots, even minor mismatches in hardware configuration relative to pretraining can cause severe performance drops. Finetuning the VLA on in-domain expert data from the new embodiment improves performance on the expert task but leads to a loss in its original instruction following and behavioral priors. In this paper, we propose a self-supervised method that generates online interaction rollouts from the zero-shot VLA as additional training data for finetuning. Our experiments show this finetuning scheme yields strong multi-task policies that, on the target robot, (1) inherit prior tasks distilled from the zero-shot model, (2) enable generalist instruction following, while (3) learning new skills from expert data with improved sample efficiency. We demonstrate the success of our approach across test sets probing generalization on a real ALOHA robot and a new simulation benchmark in RoboTwin. Video results are available at https://self-supervised-control.pages.dev/

</details>


### [136] [LF-GICP: Parameter-Free Degeneracy-Aware LiDAR Odometry via a Voxel-Normal Localizability Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19522)
*Eunsoo Im*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LF-GICP，一种免参数调节的扫描到地图LiDAR里程计退化处理方法。通过正则化无关的体素法向局部化场及其两种统计量（方向各向异性分数f₀和信息质量λ₀），结合时序中值门控触发Fisher信息对应加权，在KITTI、GEODE隧道、MulRan和HeLiPR数据集上均取得领先精度，且无需针对环境重新调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何退化环境（如隧道、走廊）中，扫描到地图LiDAR里程计会在不可观测轴上产生无界漂移，而现有退化处理方法需要针对不同环境进行参数调节，缺乏通用性。

Method: 1）揭示体素化GICP中GN Hessian会掩盖平移退化问题，因为协方差正则化使平移块人为地保持良好的条件数；2）提出免正则化的体素法向局部化场，并提取两种统计量：归一化分数f₀检测方向各向异性，绝对每体素质量λ₀区分信息缺失（隧道）与信息稀释（密集开放场景）；3）结合时序中值门控，触发Fisher信息对应加权机制。

Result: 在统一评估协议下，LF-GICP在KITTI上取得最低相对平移误差（0.865%），在GEODE隧道和MulRan数据集上优于基线，在HeLiPR上均值领先，且四种传感器类型上无需重新调参即可泛化。此外实验证明直线均匀隧道沿其轴向对仅LiDAR配准而言不可观测。

Conclusion: LF-GICP通过固定规则的一次性标定（仅基于两段短序列）即可通用地处理LiDAR里程计的几何退化问题，跨多样化环境和传感器类型均表现优异，同时从理论上证实了特定退化场景的固有不可观测性。

Abstract: Scan-to-map LiDAR odometry drifts unboundedly along the unobservable axes of geometrically degenerate environments like tunnels and corridors, and existing degeneracy handling requires environment-specific parameter tuning. This paper presents a parameter-free approach. We show that in voxelized GICP the Gauss--Newton (GN) Hessian masks translational degeneracy, because covariance regularization keeps the translation block artificially well-conditioned. We bypass this with a regularization-free voxel-normal localizability field and two of its statistics: a normalized fraction $f_0$ detecting directional anisotropy, and an absolute per-voxel mass $λ_0$ distinguishing information absence (tunnels) from dilution (dense open scenes). A temporal-median gate combines both to trigger Fisher-information correspondence weighting. Calibrated once by fixed rules on two short sequences and then frozen, LF-GICP achieves the lowest KITTI relative translation error ($0.865\%$) under an identical evaluation protocol against re-run baselines, outperforms them on GEODE tunnels and MulRan, leads the HeLiPR mean, and generalizes across four sensor types without re-tuning. We further demonstrate empirically that straight, uniform tunnels remain unobservable along their axis for LiDAR-only registration.

</details>


### [137] [Multimodal Trajectory Planning for Surface Vehicles using Turning Circle-based Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19537)
*Changyu Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自主水面船舶的无引导路径多模态轨迹规划框架，将模型预测控制（MPC）与基于转向圆的控制障碍函数（TC-CBF）相结合，通过区分左转和右转避碰模式，在动态环境中实现高效、安全的轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于欧氏距离的控制障碍函数（ED-CBF）仅依据距离远近评估安全性，忽略了水面船舶的非完整运动学和有限转向能力，导致单模式MPC面临局部极小值和死锁问题，且多数多模态规划方法依赖全局引导路径。

Method: 提出TC-CBF框架，利用转向圆的几何特性构建可行避让区域，生成左转和右转两种不同的避碰模式，将避让方向直接嵌入安全约束中，使优化求解器无需全局引导路径即可探索拓扑上不同的轨迹。

Result: 在多个移动船舶的仿真实验中，与单模式基线相比，所提方法在各类交通密度下均实现了更高的任务成功率、更少的安全违规次数和更小的残余违规量。

Conclusion: 该框架有效缓解了单模式MPC的局部极小值和死锁问题，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了动态环境下自主水面船舶的避碰安全性和任务成功率。

Abstract: This paper presents a guide path-free multimodal trajectory planning framework for autonomous surface vehicles operating in dynamic environments. The proposed method integrates model predictive control (MPC) with a turning circle-based control barrier function (TC-CBF). Unlike conventional Euclidean distance-based CBFs (ED-CBFs), which evaluate safety solely based on proximity, the TC-CBF accounts for the nonholonomic motion and finite turning capability of a surface vehicle. Its geometric formulation identifies feasible avoidance regions according to the vehicle's turning circles and generates distinct left- and right-turning avoidance modes. These modes allow the optimization solver to explore and select topologically different trajectories without relying on globally planned guide paths, as required by many conventional multimodal planning approaches. By embedding the avoidance direction directly into the safety constraint, the proposed framework alleviates the local-minimum and deadlock problems of single-mode MPC while maintaining computational efficiency. Extensive simulations involving multiple moving vessels demonstrate that the proposed method achieves higher success rates, fewer safety violations, and smaller residual violations than single-mode baselines across all tested traffic densities.

</details>


### [138] [HiTac-WAM: A Hierarchical Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19574)
*Chao Xue,Chaofan Zhang,Wenxuan Ma,Guocai Yao,Shaowei Cui,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HiTac-WAM，一种分层触觉世界动作模型，在动作执行前预测未来触觉状态（接触状态、3D形变场和滑移风险），通过有向分层架构组织触觉预测，用于机器人操作中的动作规划与重规划，在真实机器人上将成功率从31.1%提升至72.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉感知的世界模型通常将未来触觉表示为图像或潜在流，没有建模触觉状态在物理上存在的层次依赖关系（如接触→形变→滑移的因果链条），这限制了预测的准确性和物理一致性。

Method: HiTac-WAM将触觉预测分解为接触状态、3D形变场和滑移风险三个层级，形成一个有向层级结构，下游阶段条件化于上游阶段的stop-gradient信号。采用有向注意力掩码，允许触觉查询关注每个候选动作的视频-动作上下文，但阻止视频和动作查询关注触觉token。规划时为候选动作chunk排序，执行时将触觉预测作为参考，检测预测与实际触觉状态的持续偏差以触发重规划。

Result: 接触F1达到0.921；在相同训练预算下，有向层级使3D位移L2误差降低17.6%，滑移AUPRC提升60.4%。在芯片抓取、黑板擦除和USB插入任务中，层级预测引导的选择将平均真实机器人成功率从31.1%提升至61.1%，完整系统达到72.2%。

Conclusion: 有向分层触觉预测建模物理层级依赖关系能够显著提升触觉世界模型的预测精度和机器人操作成功率，验证了在触觉状态中显式建模层次结构对于世界模型的有效性。

Abstract: World action models jointly predict future visual observations and actions, whereas existing tactile-aware variants typically represent future touch as an image or latent stream without modeling the physical dependencies that organize tactile states hierarchically. We present HiTac-WAM, a hierarchical tactile world action model that forecasts a sequence of future tactile states for each candidate action chunk before execution. The forecast factorizes into contact state, a 3D deformation field, and slip risk, organized as a directed hierarchy in which each downstream stage is conditioned on stop-gradient signals from preceding stages. A directed attention mask allows tactile queries to attend to the video-action context of each candidate while preventing video and action queries from attending to tactile tokens. For planning, HiTac-WAM ranks candidate action chunks using tactile forecasts and task-progress estimates. For execution, the selected tactile forecast is retained as a reference; persistent discrepancies between predicted and observed tactile states trigger corrective replanning. HiTac-WAM achieves a mean contact F1 of 0.921; under matched training budgets, the directed hierarchy reduces 3D displacement L2 error by 17.6% relative to the deformation-only predictor and improves slip AUPRC by 60.4% relative to the slip-only predictor. Across chip grasping, blackboard erasing, and USB insertion, selection guided by the hierarchical forecasts increases the average real-robot success rate from 31.1% to 61.1%, while the full system attains 72.2%.

</details>


### [139] [OrthoSkillVLA: Continual Skill Learning via Gradient-Informed Skill Subspace Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19589)
*Jiaqi Wang,Zhou Fang,Qiongfeng Shi,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OrthoSkillVLA，一种面向预训练VLA模型的参数高效持续技能学习框架。通过对VLM和ActionHead分别施加子空间约束，并为输出层引入轻量级特征感知MoE解码器，在不回放演示数据的情况下，有效缓解灾难性遗忘，同时保持对新技能的学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA模型在依次适应多种技能时，会扰动先前技能的表征和速度映射，导致灾难性遗忘。现有方法存在局限：基于架构的方法通过隔离技能提升保留能力但增加推理开销；子空间约束方法虽最小化干扰但对整个模型施加统一约束，忽视了VLA内部组件的不同角色（VLM维护宽泛语义表征易容量耗尽，ActionHead精炼语义为局部速度模式且高度敏感）。

Method: 1) 对VLM和ActionHead分别施加独立的子空间约束，以保留可复用的语义容量并保护局部速度模式；2) 引入轻量级特征感知MoE解码器，为每个技能分配紧凑专家，通过无训练路由器根据特征空间亲和度选择专家，解决输出层冻结导致的表达瓶颈与更新导致的覆盖问题。

Result: 在广泛的仿真和真实世界评估以及消融实验中，OrthoSkillVLA在获取新技能的同时更好地保留了先前技能，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文通过识别VLA模型中VLM与ActionHead的角色差异，提出针对性的子空间约束策略，并用特征感知MoE解码器解决输出层矛盾，为持续技能学习提供了有效且参数高效的解决方案，无需演示数据回放。

Abstract: Pretrained Vision-Language-Action models provide a strong foundation for robot learning, but sequentially adapting them to diverse skills can perturb the representations and velocity mappings used by previous skills, leading to catastrophic forgetting. Architecture-based approaches improve retention by isolating skills but lead to increased inference footprint. Recent subspace-constrained methods restrict parameter updates in an orthogonal subspace to minimize interference but impose a unified constraint on the entire model. We analyze the distinct roles of internal VLA components and identify two VLA-specific challenges. First, the VLM maintains broad semantic representations, making it vulnerable to capacity exhaustion, whereas the ActionHead refines semantics into localized velocity patterns that are highly sensitive to perturbations. Second, the final velocity decoder serves as a readout layer. Freezing it forms an output-stage expressivity bottleneck, while updating it risks overwriting previous velocity mappings. To this end, we propose OrthoSkillVLA, a parameter-efficient framework for continual skill learning in pretrained VLA models without demonstration replay. Given the representation heterogeneity, we impose separate subspace constraints on the VLM and ActionHead, preserving reusable semantic capacity while protecting localized velocity patterns. For the output layer, we introduce a lightweight feature-aware MoE decoder, where each skill is allocated a compact expert and a training-free router selects the expert according to feature-space affinity. Extensive simulated and real-world evaluations, together with ablations, demonstrate that OrthoSkillVLA better preserves prior skills while acquiring new ones.

</details>


### [140] [The Verification Gap in Networked Physical AI: A Post-Semantic Communication Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19593)
*Shunsuke Saruwatari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"验证鸿沟"概念，指物理AI系统中提案虽有效但缺乏及时证据或授权而无法执行。为此引入"后语义通信框架"，将证据充分性、授权终局性分离在下游运行时门，区分证据传输（扩大终局者可及记录集）与证据协调（抑制已持有记录的冗余传输），并用有限状态机制验证评估器的一致性。受控通信实验揭示了由发送方/接收方终局性决定的不对称性。最后提出片段级报告模式以规范未来物理AI研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络化物理AI中，一个任务有效的提案并不等同于合理的物理行动——即使提案本身可理解且有效，但及时的证据或授权缺失会导致行动无法最终落实，这一"验证鸿沟"是当前物理AI系统接口设计中被忽视的关键问题。

Method: 提出后语义通信框架：从应用声明的证据需求出发，将合格观测表示为证据记录，通过同一路径验证支持性与冲突性记录，并分离证据充分性与授权终局性于下游运行时门；区分证据转移（扩大终局者可及记录集）与证据协调（抑制已持有记录的冗余传输）；利用有限状态机框架检查验证评估器是否一致实现所述区分。

Result: 受控通信实验显示终局者依赖的不对称性：发送方终局化的反馈通过证据转移实现全可行区域的证据可达性扩展；接收方终局化的反馈则利用协调抑制冗余载荷，直至丢失、延迟、新鲜度与截止时间成本将选择转向单向通信。片段级报告模式定义了未来物理AI研究的共同基准。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性地将提案形成与物理执行之间的验证鸿沟形式化，提出的框架为物理AI系统中证据验证、授权终结与通信协调提供了统一的理论基础与分析工具，并为后续量化研究建立了可复用的报告规范。

Abstract: A task-effective proposal is not yet a justified physical action. In networked Physical AI, a proposal may be understood while valid, timely, proposal-bound evidence or the authority required to finalize an action remains unavailable. We call this mismatch the verification gap and introduce a Post-Semantic Communication Framework for the systems interface between proposal formation and physical execution. The framework begins with application-declared evidence requirements, represents qualifying observations as evidence records, validates supporting and conflicting records through one path, and separates evidence sufficiency from authorized finalization and a downstream runtime gate. It further distinguishes evidence transfer, which can enlarge the record set reachable by a finalizer, from evidence coordination, which can suppress transmission around records already held at the finalization endpoint. Finite-state framework checks verify that the evaluator implements the declared distinctions consistently. Under the declared model, the controlled communication study exposes a finalizer-dependent asymmetry: sender-finalized Feedback uses evidence transfer to expand evidence reachability throughout the feasible plotted region, whereas receiver-finalized Feedback uses coordination to suppress redundant payload until loss, latency, freshness, and deadline costs shift selection to One-way. Finally, an episode-level reporting schema defines common denominators for future measured Physical-AI studies.

</details>


### [141] [What Matters for Latent Actions in Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19613)
*Xizhou Bu,Qingda Hu,Lei Zhou,Lingfeng Zhang,Yingbo Tang,Zihao Liu,Xinyi Tao,Zhiqiang Ma,Qingqiu Huang,Chufeng Tang,Hongbo Wang,Jing Zhang,Jiayi Ma,Hangjun Ye,Wei Li,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次对机器人操作中的隐式动作学习进行了全面的实证研究，将41种设计选择统一到一个共同的自编码框架中，系统评估了隐式动作建模范式、学习目标与正则化方法以及隐式动作集成策略三大维度，并在三个基准和一个真实世界任务上验证了微调VLM骨干网络对下游策略学习的初始化优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LAM研究高度碎片化，不同方法在隔离且不一致的实验条件下评估各自的设计选择，导致难以识别真正决定下游机器人操作性能的关键因素。

Method: 将代表性LAM方法统一到共同的自编码框架中，系统研究41种设计选择，涵盖隐式动作建模范式、学习目标与正则化方法、隐式动作集成策略三个维度；同时考察四种代理指标以评估其预测下游操作性能的能力。

Result: 通过三个广泛使用的基准上的大量实验，提供强有力的经验证据表明：使用隐式动作微调VLM骨干网络能为下游策略学习提供更强的初始化，并在真实世界机器人操作任务上进一步验证了该结论。

Conclusion: 全面揭露了LAM各设计选择对下游操作性能的影响，并实证支持VLM骨干微调策略作为LAM方法中的有效初始化途径。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling robot learning to leverage large-scale unlabeled videos through latent actions that serve as compact surrogates for physical actions. Despite rapid progress, research on LAM remains highly fragmented, with existing methods evaluating different design choices in isolation under inconsistent experimental settings, making it difficult to identify the factors that truly determine downstream robotic manipulation performance. In this work, we present the first comprehensive empirical study of latent action learning for robotic manipulation. We unify representative LAM methods within a common autoencoding framework and systematically investigate 41 LAM design choices across three dimensions, including latent action modeling paradigms, learning objectives and regularization methods, and latent action integration strategies. We further examine four proxy metrics for evaluating latent action quality and assess their ability to reliably predict downstream robotic manipulation performance. Extensive experiments on three widely used benchmarks provide strong empirical evidence that fine-tuning vision-language model (VLM) backbones with latent actions provides a stronger initialization for downstream policy learning, with further validation on real-world robot manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [142] [Magnetically Self-Sealed MR Haptic Actuator With PWM-Based Excitation and High-Fidelity Torque Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19635)
*Dong Qiang,Tian Yuan,Song Yang,Kequan Xia,Thomas Reddyhoff,Yikun Zhang,Cheng Cheng,Min Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成式磁流变液（MRF）触觉系统，包含磁自密封旋转执行器、低迟滞PWM驱动和高保真基于模型的扭矩渲染。实验表明在10kHz PWM下系统响应可用非线性模型描述，所提出的混合控制器相比PID将方波过冲、欠冲和稳态RMSE分别降低77.4%、61.9%和68.3%，并通过1.5小时长时间测试验证了稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确稳定的扭矩渲染对于安全、可感知的人机交互至关重要。MRF执行器虽可提供紧凑且快速可控的触觉反馈，但在实际应用中面临流体密封、低迟滞激励、精确扭矩控制和长期稳定运行等挑战。

Method: 采用磁静力学仿真优化磁性和非磁性材料的布置以聚焦磁通，实现600 N·mm/A的输出。系统在10kHz PWM下运行以降低迟滞，通过非线性模型描述响应特性，控制器结合前馈、迟滞补偿、PI反馈和滑模修正。

Result: 10kHz下系统表现出更低的迟滞和更好的可重复性。混合控制器相比PID将方波过冲降低77.4%，欠冲降低61.9%，稳态RMSE降低68.3%。系统成功跟踪正弦和生物力学模型参考信号，1.5小时测试仅产生2.5°C温升且无明显跟踪损失。

Conclusion: 该高保真扭矩渲染系统能够从根本上改变人机协作方式，使交互更安全、更高效、更直观。

Abstract: Accurate and stable torque rendering is essential for safe and perceptive human--machine interaction. Magnetorheological fluid (MRF)-based actuators offer a compact and rapidly controllable solution for haptic feedback, but their practical implementation requires reliable fluid sealing, low-hysteresis excitation, accurate torque control, and stable long-duration operation. This article presents an integrated MRF haptic system featuring a compact magnetically self-sealed rotary actuator, low-hysteresis PWM operation, high-fidelity model-based torque rendering, and stable performance during long-time operation. Magnetostatic simulation guides the arrangement of magnetic and nonmagnetic materials to focus flux in the multidisk torque and permanent-magnet sealing regions, enabling a maximum 600 N$\cdot$mm/A output. Experiments show that higher PWM frequencies reduce hysteresis and improve repeatability. At 10 kHz, the response is represented by a nonlinear model that varies with the direction and speed of torque change. The real-time controller combines feedforward, hysteresis compensation, PI feedback, and sliding-mode correction. Compared with PID, it reduces square-wave overshoot, undershoot, and steady-state RMSE by 77.4\%, 61.9\%, and 68.3\%, respectively. It tracks sinusoidal and biomechanics-model-based references, and a 1.5-h test shows only a 2.5 $^\circ$C rise near the coil with no clear tracking loss. This high-fidelity torque rendering will fundamentally transform human--robot collaboration by making interactions safer, more efficient, and more intuitive.

</details>


### [143] [World-Model-Grounded LLM Planning for AUV and ASV Navigation Near Offshore Wind Farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19661)
*Markus Buchholz,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出用世界模型增强基于大语言模型的机器人规划器，通过物理接地神经世界模型、三阶段梯度轨迹优化器和带信任域的MPC闭环重规划器，解决LLM缺乏物理感知的问题。在6自由度AUV和3自由度ASV两个海上平台、五个基准任务中均实现零碰撞，并在GazeboSim仿真中相比无接地基线将目标距离误差降低70-93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能将自然语言任务转化为机器人动作序列，但缺乏物理感知能力，无法判断命令应执行多长时间，也无法预测是否会导致机器人漂移撞上障碍物，需要引入世界模型弥补这一不足。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 物理接地的神经世界模型；(2) 三阶段基于梯度的轨迹优化器；(3) 带信任域守卫的MPC式闭环重规划器。语言模型决定"做什么"，世界模型决定"做多久"，适用于6自由度推进器驱动的AUV和3自由度差速驱动的ASV。此外，ASV还配备VLM辅助的语义映射管线，从卫星图像、海图和天气预报API提取障碍物与环境上下文。

Result: 在两个平台各五个基准任务中，所有目标均达成且预测碰撞为零。迁移到GazeboSim（含洋流、波浪和推进器动力学）后保持无碰撞，目标距离误差相比无接地基线降低70-82%（ASV）和约93%（AUV）。残差微调后，替代rollout的RMSE分别降低60%（AUV）和69%（ASV）。VLM语义映射管线作为手工障碍物几何的替代方案，导航准确率达96%。

Conclusion: 物理接地的世界模型能有效扩展LLM规划器的能力，弥补LLM缺乏物理感知的缺陷，在真实海洋环境仿真中显著提升任务完成精度与碰撞安全性，且VLM辅助语义映射可替代手动障碍物定义，提升系统实用性。

Abstract: Large language models can turn a natural-language mission into a sequence of robot actions, but they do not have a sense of physics: they cannot judge how long a command should run, or whether it will make the robot drift into an obstacle. We proposed the use of a world model to expand the capabilities of Large Language model-based planners. Our method has three components: a physics-grounded neural world model, a three-phase gradient-based trajectory optimizer, and a Model Predictive Controller (MPC)-style closed-loop replanner with a trust-region guard. The language model decides what to do, and the world model decides how long, whether that means driving eight thrusters through 6 DOF or two differential thrusters through 3 DOF. We evaluate two marine vehicle classes operating near offshore wind infrastructure: a 6-DOF Autonomous Underwater Vehicle (AUV) and a 3-DOF differential-drive Autonomous Surface Vehicle (ASV). In five benchmark missions per platform, both vehicles reach every goal with zero predicted collisions, and both transfer to GazeboSim under ocean current, waves, and thruster dynamics, remaining collision-free and cutting GazeboSim goal-distance error versus the ungrounded baseline by 70-82% (ASV) and roughly 93% (AUV), after a residual fine-tuning pass that separately reduces surrogate rollout Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) by 60% (AUV) and 69% (ASV). For the ASV we further demonstrate a Vision language model (VLM)-assisted semantic-mapping pipeline that extracts obstacles and environmental context from satellite imagery, nautical charts, and forecast Application Programming Interface (API) instead of onboard sensors, reaching 96% navigability accuracy as a drop-in replacement for hand-specified obstacle geometry.

</details>


### [144] [SAGE: Ergodic Control for Autonomous and Adaptive Inspection of Subsea Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19671)
*Markus Buchholz,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE（语义与自适应生成遍历性）架构，用于水下采油树阀门巡检。该架构基于实时传感器推导的风险分布进行遍历控制，使载具按风险比例分配巡检时间，而非按固定脚本路线。在5个阀门的双采油树场景中，SAGE对高风险阀门的复检频率（每5.8秒）显著高于固定A*路线的均匀巡检（每8.1秒），且能无需重新规划即可对新发现的泄漏做出自适应响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下采油树阀门需要持续巡检，但不同阀门在不同时刻的风险等级不同（如疑似泄漏的阀门需要更频繁检查），且风险状态会在任务过程中随新泄漏被发现而动态变化。固定路线规划无法适应这种动态风险分布。

Method: 提出SAGE架构，采用遍历控制（ergodic control）范式。核心思想是让载具时间分配比例跟随实时传感器推导的风险分布，而非执行预设路线。遍历控制律从风险分布中推导，使载具运动模式在统计意义上与风险分布匹配。

Result: 在双采油树、5个阀门的仿真场景中：SAGE遍历控制下，主导风险阀门每5.8秒被复检一次；A*固定路线下所有阀门每8.1秒均匀复检一次。新检测到的泄漏可在下一控制周期即改变载具行为，无需显式重新规划或操作员介入。

Conclusion: 遍历控制比固定路线更适配动态风险的巡检任务：不仅能在高风险区域投入相近的总时间，还能显著提高高优先级目标的重访频率，使泄漏可能被忽视的时间缩短约三分之二；且对风险变化具有天然的自适应能力。

Abstract: Subsea Christmas Trees (XTs) are underwater structures that use valves for directing oil flow, needing constant inspection. But not every valve carries the same risk at the same time: a valve with a suspected leak needs to be revisited far more often than one with a clean history, and that risk picture changes during the mission as new leaks are found. To handle this, we present SAGE (Semantic and Adaptive Generative Ergodicity), an ergodic-control architecture that allocates vehicle time in proportion to a live, sensor-derived risk distribution rather than a scripted route. We study a two-XT scenario, with five valves in total, and compare a fixed-loop A* tour against SAGE. Both methods can be tuned to spend similar total time near a high-risk valve, but only ergodic control also checks it more often: in simulation, a dominant-risk valve was revisited every 5.8 s under ergodic control against a fixed 8.1 s for every valve under A*, regardless of risk, so a leak can go unnoticed for barely two-thirds as long. Because the tracked distribution is recomputed rather than planned once, a newly detected leak shifts vehicle behavior on the next control cycle with no explicit re-planning step and no operator in the loop, which a fixed tour cannot do without a discrete re-route. We derive the ergodic control law behind this behavior and report simulation results on the five-valve scenario.

</details>


### [145] [Keeping the Franka Emika Panda alive: a ROS 2 stack with a reliable position interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19740)
*Antonio Langella,Davide Risi,Vincenzo Petrone,Enrico Ferrentino,Pasquale Chiacchio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开源软件栈，恢复Franka Emika Panda机器人在ROS 2中的支持，并解决了其外部位置控制接口长期存在的不稳定问题。作者通过分析发现振动和保护性停机源于外部控制回路的时序和采样抖动问题，而非机器人本身限制。通过引入异步硬件接口、速率匹配机制和位置域参考生成策略，实现了可靠平滑的位置控制，并在多个应用中验证了该方案的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Franka Emika Panda机器人的外部位置控制接口长期存在不稳定问题（振动、保护性停机），且缺乏现代ROS 2生态支持，阻碍了安全、响应式、可复现的人机协作应用的开发。

Method: 1）分析外部位置控制不稳定的根本原因，指出问题源于控制回路时序和采样抖动而非机器人硬件；2）引入异步硬件接口，将实时通信与ROS 2控制回路解耦；3）设计速率匹配机制以适配较慢的命令源；4）提出位置域参考生成策略，生成可靠平滑的位置指令。

Result: 实验证明该架构能可靠跟踪速度参考，减少官方实现引入的运动伪影；该软件栈已在运动规划、柔顺控制、位置控制操作和触觉遥操作等应用中，于两个独立Panda平台上得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作恢复了Panda机器人现代且可靠的ROS 2生态，降低了开发集成规划、感知、交互和共享自主性的安全人机协作应用的门槛，并提供开源代码和视频资源供社区使用。

Abstract: This paper presents an open-source software stack that restores ROS 2 support for the Franka Emika Panda robot while resolving the long-standing unreliability of its external position control interface. We first analyze the root causes of unstable position control and show that the observed vibrations and protective stops arise from the timing of the external control loop and sampling jitter, rather than from limitations of the robot itself. Building on this analysis, we introduce an asynchronous hardware interface that decouples real-time communication from the ROS 2 control loop, a rate-matching mechanism for slower command sources, and a position-domain reference generation strategy that produces reliable, smooth position commands. Experimental validation shows that the proposed architecture reliably tracks velocity references by reducing motion artifacts introduced by the official implementation, and the stack is validated across motion planning, compliance control, position-controlled manipulation, and haptic teleoperation on two independent Panda platforms. By restoring a modern, reliable, and open ROS 2 ecosystem for the Panda, this work lowers the barrier to developing safe, responsive, and reproducible human-robot collaboration applications that integrate planning, perception, interaction, and shared autonomy. Code and videos are available on our website at https://sites.google.com/view/fer-ros2/.

</details>


### [146] [GOAG: Generative and Object-Agnostic Grasp Planner for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19759)
*Julien Merand,Boris Meden,Mathieu Grossard,Liming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GOAG，一种基于生成模型且与物体无关的多指抓取规划器。核心洞察是夹持器与物体在接触点处具有相同的表面几何，因此在推理时仅需引入物体特征即可采样有效抓取配置，无需依赖物体特定的训练数据。在MultiDex数据集上平均成功率86.93%，达到最先进水平，且处理速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度学习的多指抓取规划器通常在有限、物体特定的数据集上训练，难以泛化到新物体。作者希望突破这一限制，提出一种不依赖物体特定训练数据的抓取规划方法。

Method: 基于"夹持器与物体在接触点处表面几何相同"这一观察，学习特定夹持器接触表面分布的紧凑潜空间表征，通过深度生成模型高效采样有效抓取配置；物体特征仅在推理阶段引入，用于检索与夹持器能力兼容的可行接触区域。

Result: 在MultiDex数据集上实现86.93%的平均成功率，达到最先进水平；在生成大量抓取时处理速度显著更快，性能与专门在该数据集上训练的领先方法相当；在不同夹持器上均验证有效，包括仿真和真实场景。

Conclusion: GOAG有效解决了传统数据驱动抓取规划器的泛化难题，通过物体无关学习凸显优势，无需物体特定训练数据即可实现与专门训练方法相当甚至更优的性能。

Abstract: Multifingered grasping is a crucial robotic skill, but current deep-learning grasp planners often struggle to generalize to new objects because they are trained on limited, object-specific datasets. We introduce a fundamentally different approach, grounded in the observation that the gripper and the object share identical surface geometry at their mutual contact points. We propose GOAG: Generative and Object-Agnostic Grasp Planner for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation, a novel deep generative model that learns a compact latent representation of a specific gripper's contact surface distribution, enabling the efficient sampling of valid grasp configurations without relying on object-specific training data. We show that by introducing object features only at inference time, our model can effectively retrieve admissible contact areas that are compatible with the gripper's capabilities. We validate our approach through extensive experiments on established grasp protocols in both simulated and real-world scenarios, demonstrating its effectiveness with different grippers from the literature. Our method delivers state-of-the-art results on the objects from the MultiDex dataset, achieving an average success rate of 86.93%. It offers significantly faster processing when generating numerous grasps, while matching the performance of leading approaches specifically trained on this dataset. Unlike these methods, our approach does not rely on object-specific training data, highlighting the advantages of object-agnostic learning. It effectively addresses the generalization challenges faced by traditional data-driven grasp planners. Code and videos are available on our project website https://cea-list.github.io/goagweb/ .

</details>


### [147] [CoToGrasp: Contact-Topology-Conditioned Dexterous Grasp Synthesis via Canonical Workspace Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19776)
*Julien Merand,Boris Meden,Liming Chen,Mathieu Grossard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoToGrasp，一种全新的生成式框架，能够在不需要物体标注数据的情况下，严格基于特定接触拓扑合成多样化且稳定的灵巧抓取姿态。该方法通过特征化的规范工作空间，将局部物体特征投影到统一的抓取器中心域，实现对象无关训练和零样本泛化，在DexGraspNet数据集上取得最先进性能，并在真实机器人平台上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取规划器主要优化物理稳定性，关注能否抓住物体而非如何抓取以支持下游功能任务。但将抓取合成条件化到特定人类抓取分类需要昂贵的物体标注数据集，存在数据收集瓶颈。

Method: 提出CoToGrasp生成式框架：1）以对象无关方式训练，规避数据收集瓶颈；2）引入特征化规范工作空间，将局部物体特征投影到统一的抓取器中心域，解耦语义功能意图与任意物体几何；3）在该工作空间中学习抓取器的固有接触流形，实现对新物体的零样本泛化。

Result: 在大规模DexGraspNet数据集上的广泛评估表明，CoToGrasp达到最先进性能，超越现有分类引导的规划器；在物理机器人平台上验证了合成接触拓扑的物理可行性和运动学可行性。

Conclusion: CoToGrasp通过对象无关训练和特征化规范工作空间，成功解决了接触拓扑条件化抓取合成中的数据瓶颈问题，实现零样本泛化并完成物理验证，展示了其在实际机器人系统中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Current dexterous grasp planners primarily optimize for physical stability, focusing on whether an object can be grasped rather than how it should be grasped to support downstream functional tasks. However, conditioning grasp synthesis on specific human grasp taxonomies typically requires prohibitively expensive, object-annotated datasets. To address these limitations, we propose CoToGrasp, a novel generative framework that synthesizes diverse, stable grasps strictly conditioned on specific contact topologies. To bypass the data collection bottleneck, CoToGrasp is trained entirely in an object-agnostic manner. We introduce a feature-based canonical workspace that projects local object features into a unified gripper-centric domain, effectively decoupling the semantic functional intent from the arbitrary object geometry. By learning the intrinsic contact manifold of the gripper within this workspace, our model achieves zero-shot generalization to unseen objects at inference. Extensive evaluations on the large-scale DexGraspNet dataset demonstrate that CoToGrasp achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing taxonomy-guided planners. Finally, we demonstrate the physical viability and kinematic feasibility of our synthesized contact topologies on a physical robot platform. Code is available on our project website https://cea-list.github.io/cotograspweb/ .

</details>


### [148] [MILD: Tractable Terrain Modeling for Learning Improved Bipedal Locomotion on Deformable Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19955)
*Zeren Luo,Jiahui Zhang,Zhe Xu,Wanyue Li,Xinqi Li,Xuechao Chen,Zhangguo Yu,Annan Tang,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MILD是一个用于双足机器人在可变形地面上行走的框架，包含基于离散元法的物理接触求解器和地形感知的深度强化学习控制器，通过潜在调制和本体感觉估计实现地面的在线识别与自适应行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足机器人在灾难响应、行星探索等场景中对松软地形的行走至关重要，但现有仿真器无法捕捉此类可变形基底的空时异质性，限制了双足机器人在变形表面上的运动能力。

Method: 提出MILD框架：(1) 基于物理的离散元接触求解器，精确模拟空间变化的足-地形交互；(2) 结合潜在调制与本体感觉估计的地形感知控制器，通过深度强化学习训练得到。

Result: 与最先进方法相比，MILD在训练中生成更多样化和真实的接触场景，训练出的控制器在真实可变形表面上展现出自然适应能力；硬件实验验证了系统能在广泛的表面刚度范围内进行在线地形识别与适应。

Conclusion: MILD通过更真实的物理仿真与地形感知控制相结合，显著提升了双足机器人在松软可变形地面上的行走适应能力，并通过硬件实验验证了其在实际场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Enabling robots to walk on yielding terrain is vital for applications ranging from disaster response to planetary exploration. While bipedal robots hold immense potential, their locomotion on deformable surfaces remains limited as current simulators fail to capture the spatiotemporal heterogeneity of such yielding substrates. We present MILD, featuring a physics-grounded discrete-element contact solver that accurately simulates spatially varying foot-terrain interactions. Complementing this model, we train a terrain-aware locomotion controller via deep reinforcement learning with latent modulation and proprioceptive estimation. Quantitative comparisons against state-of-the-art methods show our approach generates more diverse and realistic contact scenarios during training, resulting in controllers that exhibit natural adaptation on real deformable surfaces. Through hardware experiments, we demonstrate the system's capability for online terrain identification and adaptation across a wide range of surface stiffness.

</details>


### [149] [PVRA: A Pointwise Key-point Voting Framework for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19968)
*Kulunu Samarawickrama,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D关键点的模块化学习框架，通过学习装配依赖关系，从RGB-D输入中推断可操作的输出，以实现渐进式装配操作的自主动手操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算机视觉已实现机器人装配操作的部分自主性，但渐进式装配的自主动手操作需要更具体的技能。通过比较分析，作者认为仅感知物体是不够的，物体中心感知必须向学习装配依赖关系发展，以预测有意义的可操作输出。

Method: 提出一个基于3D关键点的模块化学习框架，用于学习装配依赖关系并从装配场景的RGB-D输入中推断可操作输出。在装配姿态估计数据集上训练和评估该网络。

Result: 在与物体中心基线方法的对比中，使用针对渐进式装配扩展的评估指标集进行验证，该框架在装配依赖学习和可操作输出推断方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 证明学习装配依赖关系对于自主装配操作至关重要，所提出的3D关键点模块化框架是有效的解决方案，可为渐进式装配提供可操作输出。

Abstract: Modern computer vision has enabled partial autonomy in robotic assembly manipulation. However, performing autonomous manipulation of a progressive assembly demands a more specific set of skills, in addition to perceiving the objects. Through a comparative analysis of research in the associated domains, we deduce that object-centric perception must advance towards learning assembly dependencies to predict meaningful actionable outputs for autonomous assembly manipulation. Subsequently, we present a 3D keypoint-based modular learning framework to learn assembly dependencies to infer actionable outputs given a RGB-D input of an assembly scene. We train and evaluate our trained network on an assembly pose estimation dataset and compare it against object-centric baselines with an augmented set of metrics for progressive assemblies.

</details>


### [150] [Learning Highly Dynamic Skills Transition for Quadruped Jumping Through Constrained Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19977)
*Zeren Luo,Jiahui Zhang,Yimin Han,Ji Ma,Minghao Lu,Ioannis Havoutis,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层强化学习框架，使四足机器人在通过狭窄门等受限障碍物时能够执行爆发性、侵略性的动态运动。低层策略通过模仿学习模仿真实动物行为，形成多样化技能库；高层控制器结合视觉检测的门信息和低层技能能力，决策出无碰撞轨迹以动态穿越障碍。该框架还可扩展到其他高动态任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有腿动物能够在狭窄空间中执行爆发性运动，但如何在四足机器人上复现这一行为一直是长期挑战。现有方法难以实现通过受限障碍物的激进动态运动。

Method: 采用分层强化学习管道：1) 使用模仿学习训练低层策略，模仿真实动物行为并形成多样化技能集；2) 高层控制器具备低层技能的感知能力，并通过基于视觉的检测获取门信息，从而决策合适的机动方式并生成无碰撞轨迹实现动态穿越。

Result: 该框架成功使四足机器人自主完成空中穿越狭窄门这一高动态任务，并验证了框架可扩展到其他高动态任务，是地面行走机器人上首次完成自主敏捷穿越门任务的工之一。

Conclusion: 通过分层强化学习与模仿学习的结合，四足机器人的灵巧性得到显著提升，能够匹配其生物对应物的运动能力，为腿足机器人在复杂受限环境中执行动态任务提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Although legged animals are capable of performing explosive motions while traversing confined spaces, replicating this behavior in quadrupedal robots has been a longstanding challenge. Here, we propose a hierarchical reinforcement learning pipeline that empowers the robots to perform aggressive locomotion through constrained obstacles--a narrow gate. The imitation learning technique is used to train the low-level policy, which mimics the behaviors of real animals and forms a set of diverse skills. The high-level controller, having an awareness of the capability of low-level skills and acquiring the gate information via vision-based detection, determines the suitable maneuvers with collision-free trajectories to traverse it dynamically. Notably, we also verify that this framework can be extended to other highly dynamic tasks. This is one of the first works that perform autonomous and agile aerial gate traversal tasks on ground-walking robots, extending the lifelike agility of legged robots to match that of their biological counterparts.

</details>


### [151] [Evidence-Gated Task and Motion Planning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20084)
*Tsunehiko Tanaka,Matthew Stephenson,Alistair Macvicar,Edgar Simo-Serra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EAFG框架，通过VLM生成探索性子目标并结合TAMP执行来获取视觉证据，并引入可行性门控决策机制，解决部分可观测环境下机器人长时程操作任务中目标物体不确定的问题，实验证明其在烹饪任务中能提升食谱完成率并减少对缺失物体的重复操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测条件下，VLM结合TAMP的方法可能产生缺乏观测支持的子目标，依赖VLM先验知识而导致执行失败或意外结果，需要一种机制来获取证据并评估可行性。

Method: 提出EAFG框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 通过VLM生成探索性子目标并利用TAMP执行来获取视觉证据；(2) 应用可行性门控（feasibility gate）决策，在继续规划、获取更多证据或停止之间做出选择。

Result: 在烹饪任务中，对于物体使用模糊的情况，EAFG通过规划前发现任务相关物体提高了食谱完成率；对于指令要求缺失物体的场景，EAFG促进适当的停止决策，减少了对该物体的重复操作尝试。

Conclusion: EAFG通过证据获取与可行性门控的结合，有效缓解了部分可观测环境下VLM先验知识与观测事实不一致导致的问题，在提升任务完成率的同时避免了无效操作。

Abstract: Robots executing long-horizon manipulation tasks from natural-language instructions must reason about both semantic task structure and geometric feasibility. However, under partial observability, the availability of goal-relevant objects may be uncertain. In such cases, approaches that combine Vision-Language Models (VLMs) with Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) may generate subgoals that rely on the VLM's prior knowledge without observational support, leading to execution failures or unintended outcomes. We propose Evidence Acquisition and Feasibility Gating (EAFG), a framework that acquires visual evidence through VLM-generated exploratory subgoals and TAMP-based execution. EAFG then applies a feasibility gate to decide whether to proceed with task planning, acquire further evidence, or halt. Our experiments show that, in cooking tasks with ambiguous object use, EAFG improves recipe completion by discovering task-relevant objects before planning. For instructions requiring an absent object, EAFG promotes appropriate halt decisions and reduces repeated attempts to manipulate that object.

</details>


### [152] [Towards Professional Tennis Styles for Humanoid Robots with Adaptive Motion Planning and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20087)
*Tao Huang,Ruofei Liu,Xuchen Tang,Xinyin Zhang,Junli Ren,Huayi Wang,Feiyu Jia,Yukai Qi,Kangning Yin,Weishuai Zeng,Lipeng Chen,Xi Li,Ting Wu,Kailin Li,Ruoli Dai,Jingbo Wang,Lei Han,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaPT（自适应运动规划与跟踪框架），通过从广播视频学习专业网球发球和击球风格，并设计适应机制缩小sim-to-real差距，在Unitree G1和Dobos Atom两款人形机器人上实现了真实环境中的专业网球动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在球类运动中已展现潜力，但如何在保持强任务性能的同时实现专业级运动风格仍然具有挑战性。此外，仿真到现实迁移过程中跟踪性能必然下降，且自回归规划会部分忽略这一退化，噪声感知又进一步加剧了问题。

Method: AdaPT采用层次化设计：规划器生成风格化的运动学动作，跟踪器在最小干扰规划的情况下执行动作。适应机制包括：学习跟踪随机化执行速度以提升跟踪鲁棒性，以及通过学习的运动速度适配器调节规划器以缓解复合误差。

Result: 在Unitree G1上的真实实验验证了适应机制在弥合sim-to-real差距方面的有效性；进一步在全尺寸Dobos Atom人形机器人（1.7m）上部署AdaPT策略，实现了无需动作捕捉的真实环境中的发球。实验还揭示了未来人形球类运动系统的算法和工程洞见。

Conclusion: AdaPT通过层次化的规划-跟踪设计与针对性的适应机制，成功在人形机器人上实现了从广播视频学习的专业网球风格，并有效解决了sim-to-real迁移中的关键问题，为未来人形球类运动系统提供了有效方案与工程启示。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have recently demonstrated promising capabilities in real-world ball sports. However, achieving professional motion styles while maintaining strong task performance remains challenging. In this work, we propose AdaPT, an Adaptive Motion Planning and Tracking framework that learns professional tennis serving and rally styles directly from broadcast videos. This hierarchical design is motivated by the key insight that the planner generates stylistic kinematic motions, while the tracker executes them with minimal interference with planning. Despite its effectiveness in simulation, a substantial sim-to-real gap emerges: tracking performance inevitably degrades on real robots, and this degradation is partially overlooked by autoregressive planning and further compounded by noisy perception. To address these issues, our adaptation mechanism improves tracking robustness by learning to track randomized execution speeds, while conditioning the planner on a learned motion-speed adapter to mitigate compounding errors. Real-world experiments on the Unitree G1 demonstrate the effectiveness of our adaptation mechanism in bridging the sim-to-real gap. We further deploy AdaPT policies on the full-size Dobot Atom humanoid robot (1.7m) and demonstrate in-the-wild serving without motion capture. Beyond these results, our real-world experiments reveal both algorithmic and engineering insights for future humanoid ball-sports systems. Videos and code are available on our \href{https://humanoidtennis.github.io/AdaPT/}{project website}.

</details>


### [153] [Planning-Oriented End-to-End Autonomous Driving: Architectures, Evaluation, and Emerging Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20111)
*Yanchen Guan,Xingcheng Liu,Bin Rao,Chengyue Wang,Guofa Li,Yunjian Li,Lishengsa Yue,Zhiyong Cui,Chengzhong Xu,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了端到端自动驾驶从"摄像头到控制"回归向规划导向系统的演进过程，涵盖行为克隆、条件模仿学习、特权蒸馏、BEV与矢量化规划、统一感知-预测-规划架构、世界模型规划器及视觉-语言-动作系统。作者提出端到端驾驶的关键区别不在于是否使用中间表征，而在于这些表征是否被学习、监督和评估以支持安全、可行且符合路线的规划，并从四个维度（输入表征、规划输出、监督信号、评估协议）组织文献，同时分析了基准从开环轨迹匹配向闭环仿真、非反应性真实日志评估、长尾测试和人类偏好感知指标的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶领域快速发展，但存在架构多样、评估方法混乱的问题，尤其在开环轨迹匹配指标无法充分反映真实驾驶安全性的背景下，需要对现有方法进行系统化梳理，厘清该领域从回归预测向规划导向转变的关键脉络与挑战。

Method: 以结构化综述方法，对现有端到端驾驶方法按四个维度（输入表征、规划输出、监督信号、评估协议）进行分类整合，同时梳理评估基准的演进趋势，分析各种方法论之间的关联与差异。

Result: 揭示了传统开环位移指标难以支撑安全性和人类对齐性论证，强调只有通过基准一致的评估才能正确解读架构进展；总结了评估范式已从开环轨迹匹配转向闭环仿真、非反应性真实日志、长尾测试和人类偏好感知指标。

Conclusion: 提出了该领域面临的开放性挑战，包括不确定性感知规划、学习者-专家不匹配、运行时安全保证、语言-动作接地、世界模型验证以及可复现基准构建等问题，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has evolved from camera-to-control regression toward planning-oriented systems that use structured representations, trajectory-level outputs, and increasingly realistic evaluation protocols. This survey reviews this transition across behavior cloning, conditional imitation learning, privileged distillation, BEV and vectorized planning, unified perception-prediction-planning architectures, world-model-based planners, and vision-language-action systems. We argue that the key distinction in modern end-to-end driving is not whether intermediate representations are used, but whether they are learned, supervised, and evaluated to support safe, feasible, and route-compliant planning. To organize the literature, we synthesize existing methods along four axes: input representation, planning output, supervision signal, and evaluation protocol. We further examine the benchmark shift from open-loop trajectory matching to closed-loop simulation, non-reactive real-log evaluation, long-tail testing, and human-preference-aware metrics. Our analysis highlights that architectural progress is difficult to interpret without benchmark-consistent evaluation, and that displacement-based open-loop metrics alone provide limited evidence for safe and human-aligned driving. We conclude with open challenges in uncertainty-aware planning, learner-expert mismatch, runtime safety assurance, language-action grounding, world-model validation, and reproducible benchmarking.

</details>


### [154] [Video2DoorTraversal: Push Door Traversal via Simulated Door Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20251)
*Xincheng Tang,Yiji Chen,Youhan Xie,Wanyu Li,Zhengjie Shu,Lai Jiang,Wenkang Hu,Yitong Li,Jinchuang Zhang,Xibin Song,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Video2DoorTraversal，一种基于单视频的真实到仿真再到真实（real-to-sim-to-real）框架，用于轮腿式移动机械臂的开门与穿越这一长时间跨度的移动操控任务。系统通过DoorTwin重建仿真门模型，在仿真中迭代生成可执行的演示，并训练ArticuACT策略实现协调的底盘-手臂控制，最终在真实场景中实现96.57%的平均成功率及80.95%的零样本泛化成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开门与穿越是一项长时程的移动操控任务，涉及精确的门把手交互以及底盘与手臂的协调控制，具有较高的复杂性和挑战性。

Method: 该方法包含三个关键组件：（1）DoorTwin，从单一RGB视频中重建与真实实例对齐、带铰接且可仿真的门孪生模型；（2）仿真在环代理，将恢复的铰接结构转化为参数化技能程序，并通过迭代优化失败的rollout生成物理可行的演示；（3）ArticuACT，双深度策略网络，利用机器人中心摄像头条件和交互感知监督，预测协调的底盘、手臂和夹爪指令。所有感知和策略推理均在机载运行。

Result: 在五扇真实门上达到96.57%的平均成功率，对结构相似未见门实现80.95%的零样本成功率，完整接近-开门-穿越流程平均耗时约13秒。

Conclusion: 该方法通过单视频实现从仿真到真实的高效迁移，在保持高成功率的同时具备一定的零样本泛化能力，验证了real-to-sim-to-real框架在移动操控任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Door opening and traversal is a long-horizon loco-manipulation task that requires precise handle interaction and coordinated base-arm control. We present Video2DoorTraversal, a single-video real-to-sim-to-real framework for wheel-legged mobile manipulators. Given one RGB video of a real door, DoorTwin reconstructs an instance-aligned, articulated, and simulation-ready door twin with realistic geometry and appearance. A simulation-in-the-loop agent converts the recovered articulation into a parameterized skill program and iteratively refines failed rollouts to generate physically executable demonstrations. These demonstrations are used to train ArticuACT, a dual-depth policy that predicts coordinated base, arm, and gripper commands using robot-centric camera conditioning and interaction-aware supervision. With all perception and policy inference running onboard, the system achieves a 96.57% average success rate across five real doors and an 80.95% zero-shot success rate on structurally similar unseen doors, while completing the full approach, opening, and traversal sequence in approximately 13s on average. Project Page: https://video2doortraversal.github.io/.

</details>


### [155] [DART-S: Reachability-Audited Active-Suspension Preconditioning for Off-Road Vehicle Jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20275)
*Yu Hu,Fangzhou Zhao,Liang Chen,Chen Min,Wei Li,Mingyuan Sang,Jiajia Ma,Shican Chen,Di Pang,Baolei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DART-S通过在起飞前施加斜坡面悬挂预调节，改变俯仰角、俯仰速率和车轮自旋，从而在不依赖空中扭矩反应的情况下扩展姿态恢复能力。通过局部标定映射、支持感知选择器和精确配对审计，在600次BeamNG测试中实现了所有查询分支的精确执行，并在边界条件下取得显著优于基线DART的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中扭矩反应在车轮角动量预算内无法挽回起飞错误，需要一种能在起飞前提前干预姿态状态的新方法，以扩展剩余的授权预算并提高着陆姿态恢复的成功率。

Method: DART-S采用三项核心机制：(1) 斜坡面悬挂预调节来改变起飞前的俯仰角、俯仰速率和车轮自旋；(2) 局部标定映射预测每种悬挂行动如何重塑状态-预算对；(3) 支持感知选择器结合预测偏移、局部结果证据和区间可达性筛选，配合精确配对审计报告剩余权限。

Result: 在72个独立BeamNG会话中的600次新运行中，所有正、负和边界查询均遵循预设分支。在确认的40°/13 m/s边界处，DART-S实现24/24着陆后姿态准则成功，而DART为0/24（会话级Holm调整p=0.0234）。在11.5 m/s下，0.35秒时序动作实现23/24对静态预设的0/24（p=0.0156）。200 rad/s命令防护在所有600次运行中保持传动系硬限制超限为零。

Conclusion: DART-S通过起飞前预调节显著提升了姿态恢复能力，在关键边界条件下实现了从0%到100%的成功率提升，且保障了传动系安全，展示了悬挂预调节作为空中扭矩反应补充策略的有效性。

Abstract: Airborne torque reaction cannot recover takeoff errors beyond the wheel angular-momentum budget. DART-S applies ramp-face suspension preconditioning to change pitch, pitch rate, and wheel spin before liftoff, thereby shifting the queried state and altering the remaining authority budget. To predict how each suspension action reshapes this state-budget pair, DART-S employs a local calibration map. A support-aware selector combines the predicted shift with local outcome evidence and an interval-reachability screen; an exact-pair audit reports residual authority. Across 600 new runs in 72 independent BeamNG sessions, every positive, negative, and boundary query follows its prespecified branch. At the confirmed 40°/13 m/s boundary, DART-S attains 24/24 post-touchdown attitude-criterion successes versus 0/24 for DART (session-level Holm-adjusted p=0.0234). At 11.5 m/s, a 0.35 s timing action attains 23/24 versus 0/24 for the static preset (p=0.0156). The 200 rad/s command guard keeps drivetrain hard-limit exceedance at zero across all 600 runs. The source code will be available at https://github.com/MeridianCAS/DART-S

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于监督机器学习（LSTM循环神经网络）的直升机起飞重量估算模型，利用空客全球在役机队的大规模数据集训练，并按照EASA机器学习应用概念文件和EUROCAE ED-324标准执行学习保证流程。该模型已成功部署在传统航空电子计算机上，适用于机载警报等关键功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直升机起飞重量是飞行安全和机载告警等关键功能的重要参数，传统估算方法可能不够精确或难以适应复杂飞行条件。随着机器学习在航空领域的应用需求增长，需要开发符合适航审定框架的新型重量估算模型。

Method: 采用有监督机器学习方法，基于空客全球在役机队的广泛数据集训练LSTM（长短期记忆）循环神经网络。遵循EASA概念文件和EUROCAE ED-324标准，提出一套机器学习需求集和模型描述文档，并在传统航空电子计算机上完成实现与验证。

Result: 成功实现了直升机起飞重量估算的LSTM模型，所有机器学习需求在实现层面得到验证，并且证明该模型部署在传统机载航电计算机上是可行的，满足机载关键功能的应用要求。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了机器学习模型在航空关键系统中的可部署性和可靠性，为未来机载机器学习应用提供了符合适航监管框架的参考路径，尤其在重量估算和机载告警等安全关键场景中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [157] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的三角模糊数距离度量d_TR，该度量将线性重缩放直接整合到距离计算中，从而解决了传统距离度量在异构属性和不同尺度下需要预处理归一化的问题，并证明了其满足度量性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的决策常涉及不精确或不确定信息，通常用三角模糊数（TFN）表示。现有许多模糊距离度量假设所有值都在同一尺度上，当应用于具有不同尺度或单位的异构属性时需要预先归一化，这增加了流程复杂性且可能引入误差。

Method: 提出了三角模糊重缩放距离（d_TR），其核心创新是将线性重缩放（LRE）直接整合到距离计算过程中，使模糊数比较时自动进行归一化。论文正式证明了d_TR满足度量的四个性质（非负性、同一性、对称性和三角不等式），并证明了其有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性，同时引入了权重向量以优先考虑不同维度。

Result: d_TR被证明是一个有效的度量，具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性等优良性质，结合维度权重向量后适用于异构模糊数据的多种应用场景。

Conclusion: d_TR凭借其集成归一化的设计，适合应用于合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法以及多准则决策辅助等涉及异构模糊数据的场景。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [158] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Holtercare-23K大规模动态心电图多模态数据集和Holtercare-Bench基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在长时程心电信号上的时序推理与诊断报告生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在医学应用中主要处理静态图像或短期信号，而动态心电图需要复杂的时间推理和诊断报告生成，但缺乏高质量数据集和基准。

Method: 构建包含22,980个QA对的Holtercare-23K数据集，源自788条临床Holter记录，创新性地实现信号-视频-文本三模态对齐；在此基础上设计Holtercare-Bench评估基准，覆盖时序定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个维度。

Result: 主流MLLMs的零样本评估显示在处理超长病理序列时存在显著性能差距，但微调代表性模型后可获得大幅改进。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了当前MLLMs在电生理学领域的局限性，为长期医学MLLMs提供了基础性基准。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [159] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了量子-经典混合机器学习方法在基于cfDNA片段组学和DNA甲基化的肺癌检测中的应用，通过多种特征编码和纠缠策略构建量子核模型，并与经典SVM基线比较，发现量子核模型在片段组学数据上表现有竞争力，而在甲基化数据上经典SVM仍占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌筛查虽有低剂量CT等手段，但受限于筛查参与率、依从性和管理挑战。基于cfDNA的生物标志物提供互补方案，但肺癌异质性和高维非线性分子信号使早期检测仍然困难，需要探索更强大的机器学习方法。

Method: 使用量子-经典混合机器学习框架：先进行特征选择得到20和40维特征子集，将特征通过角度和稠密角度特征映射编码到量子Hilbert空间并采用多种纠缠策略，使用精确态矢模拟计算基于保真度的量子核，再集成预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归，与基于原始特征的SVM基线比较。

Result: 在重复留出验证中，量子核模型在两个数据集上均达到有竞争力的性能。片段组学方面，多个20特征配置相比经典SVM基线提升了AUC；甲基化方面，经典SVM获得最高AUC，但部分量子模型仍有竞争力且在某些情况下提高了特异性。特征数从20增至40并未一致提升性能，反而增加了变异性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法作为cfDNA肺癌检测有前景的方法，编码与纠缠设计对分类性能有系统性影响，但量子模型在不同分子数据类型上的优势存在差异，需要进一步优化。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [160] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用数据集来增强LLM不确定性量化（UQ）的方法：使用现有零样本不确定性分数以及相似查询的正确性作为特征，构建简单分类器来预测LLM响应的正确性，从而在计算开销极小的前提下持续超越现有UQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有的LLM不确定性量化方法（如基于言语化置信度或多轮生成的方法）是零样本的、无需标注数据即可使用，但在实际部署前，用户总是需要在一个特定数据集上评估其表现。作者认为可以充分利用这个数据集来提升不确定量化的性能。

Method: 构建简单的分类器来预测LLM响应的正确性，分类器的特征包括：(1)现有的不确定性分数；(2)相似查询的正确性信息。该方法通过利用部署前可获得的评估数据集来训练分类器，从而增强不确定性量化。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多种现有不确定性分数基础上均能持续取得更好的性能，且计算开销极低，是一种廉价且简单的增强方案。

Conclusion: 利用部署前的评估数据集（通过构建基于现有UQ分数和相似查询正确性的简单分类器）可以一致性地提升LLM不确定性量化的效果，且该方法不会引入显著计算开销，适用于实际部署场景。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [161] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"机制断层扫描"（mechanistic tomography）框架，将patching、梯度、Hessian-向量乘积和子集干预等机械可解释性方法统一为y=Ax+w的测量模型，并提供从低成本测量出发、逐步扩展测量族的实用流程，通过多个模型实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性领域存在多种测量方法（patching、梯度、Hessian-向量乘积、子集干预等），它们在不同访问假设下测量不同目标量，但缺乏统一的理论框架来理解其共享的测量结构，导致方法选择缺乏系统性指导。

Method: 提出通用测量形式y=Ax+w，其中A描述干预方式，x是目标映射，w包含非线性响应、采样误差和基失配。给出实用流程：先采用最低成本测量，在预期尺度上对留出干预进行测试，校准简单失配，当出现结构化残差时扩展测量族。实验涵盖两HMM控制任务、Tracr模型和GPT-2-small/Qwen-2.5-7B大模型。

Result: 两HMM中控制误差随观测器误差上升，稀疏聚合测量比坐标patching更高效；梯度访问下有限探针改善局部归因映射；提拉测量和HVP能恢复一阶映射遗漏的交互；GPT-2-small IOI中Name Mover-Negative Name Mover交互是最大的留出预测项；Qwen-2.5-7B上加性拒绝响应映射经有限校准后即足够，无需成对提拉。

Conclusion: 机制断层扫描为机械可解释性提供了统一的测量框架和实用流程，方法族的选择取决于所研究的模型基和任务复杂度——简单模型可能不需要高阶交互，而复杂模型则需要根据留出误差判断何时扩展测量族。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [162] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于模板的神经网络鲁棒性验证加速技术，提出联合稳定神经元这一新指标来解释模板子集率在不同场景下的差异，并设计了FastCert技术实现模板的自动分配与选择，在L0验证任务上取得了1.13倍的平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模板重用（template reuse）技术通过复用中间层抽象状态加速不完整验证，但其有效性在不同网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法下的鲁棒性尚不清楚，需要系统性研究其适用边界。

Method: 首先进行系统性实证研究，分析模板子集率在不同场景下的变化；提出联合稳定神经元（jointly stable neurons）这一新指标来解释模板加速效果的波动；在此基础上设计FastCert，自动将模板分配到神经网络各层以提升性能影响，并在模板不太可能带来加速时完全舍弃模板。

Result: 模板子集率在不同场景下差异显著；基于覆盖设计（covering-design）的大规模L0验证任务中，FastCert相比现有模板重用技术实现了平均1.13倍的加速。

Conclusion: 模板加速技术并非在所有场景下都有效，需要通过指标预测其适用性并自适应调整策略；FastCert通过自动分配和选择性使用模板，验证了该思路的有效性。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [163] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DCP框架，一种基于纵向贝叶斯学习的连续疾病严重程度估计方法，利用纵向弥散张量成像（DTI）数据，通过低维概率潜变量联合纵向观测与弱临床监督，推导出疾病连续评分（DCS），在ADNI队列上优于现有疾病进展方法，并验证了其临床相关性、纵向演化保持和未来转换预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病（AD）本质上是连续性的生物学过程，但现有基于神经影像的AI方法大多局限于横断面影像的离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法捕捉疾病的连续进展特性。因此需要一种能够连续量化疾病严重程度的方法。

Method: 提出DCP（Disease Continuum Positioning）框架：将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜变量，通过纵向贝叶斯学习框架整合纵向DTI观测与弱临床监督，从而推导出带有不确定性的疾病连续评分（DCS）。

Result: 在ADNI队列上的大量实验表明：DCP在疾病进展方法上表现优于代表性基线；DCS能准确刻画疾病严重程度、展现强临床相关性、保留纵向疾病演化信息，并能预测未来疾病转换。

Conclusion: DCS提供了超越传统诊断标签和临床评分的定量影像衍生表征，实现了AD进展的连续评估，有望为AD的个性化监测和治疗评估提供新的工具。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [164] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EventTime，一个多分辨率框架，用于预测网络安全事件披露后的短期异常金融损失。该框架结合市场长期上下文、事件前短期动态和事件元数据，并引入动态对比学习目标缓解稀疏监督问题。作者还构建了SECURE真实数据集，实验证明EventTime在估计事件后金融损失方面优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全事件等外部冲击通过网络传播会突然扰乱金融时间序列并造成重大异常损失，但现有时间序列预测模型主要关注趋势、季节性和自相关等内生规律，难以处理罕见且异质的外部事件。此外，稀疏的高影响力事件和背景市场噪声进一步加剧了这一挑战。

Method: EventTime采用多分辨率框架，包含三个核心组件：长视野市场上下文、短视野事件前动态和事件元数据。它引入事件融合模块，将时间表示与事件属性耦合以识别相关近期市场模式。为解决稀疏监督问题，引入动态对比学习目标，在训练期间构造事件和时间序列感知的正负样本对。同时构建了SECURE数据集，将网络安全事件与股票市场时间序列及结构化/LLM派生语义特征对齐。

Result: 实验表明EventTime在估计事件后金融损失方面持续优于最先进的时间序列和事件感知基线。进一步分析显示其具有更强的事件敏感表示、对不完整元数据更高的鲁棒性，以及更可解释的短期市场影响估计。

Conclusion: EventTime有效解决了事件条件下的影响预测问题，通过结合多分辨率时间信息和事件元数据，并利用动态对比学习缓解稀疏监督，为网络安全事件披露后的短期金融影响估计提供了更准确和可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>


### [165] [LLM as Detector: An In-context Learning Approach for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19463)
*Tu Anh Hoang Nguyen,Dang Nguyen,Thuc Duy Le,Trung Le,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Detector框架，利用LLM的上下文学习能力，将表格数据的正常状态知识（统计摘要、因果依赖、蒸馏原型）组织为提示词生成评分引擎，实现对异常样本的检测，在24个数据集上优于15个SOTA基线，且无需微调或神经网络训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的异常检测具有挑战性，因为异常样本通常表现为跨特征依赖关系的违反，而非简单的边缘偏差。现有检测器依赖几何或重建信号，先前的LLM方法主要通过对正常样本微调或生成合成异常，缺乏利用LLM上下文学习能力进行结构化评分合成的方案。

Method: 将正常训练数据转换为统计摘要、因果依赖和蒸馏原型，组织成提示词用于代码生成，生成的评分引擎综合评估统计偏差、结构不一致性和基于密度的异常，为每个测试样本计算异常分数。该方法无需LLM微调或神经网络训练。

Result: 在24个表格数据集上与15个SOTA基线对比，在混合类型和连续型数据集上均取得一致改进，同时降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: LLM-Detector通过提示词驱动的评分合成框架，消除了对LLM微调和神经网络训练的需求，实现了实际表格系统中的高效异常检测，具有良好的实用性和性能优势。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations. Existing detectors rely on geometric or reconstruction signals, while prior LLM-based approaches mainly fine-tune LLMs with normal samples or generate synthetic anomalies. We propose LLM-Detector, a framework that utilizes the in-context learning capacity of LLMs for structured, prompt-conditioned scoring synthesis, enabling LLMs to derive anomaly detection logic from structured normal-state knowledge. Specifically, normal training data are converted into statistical summaries, causal dependencies, and distilled prototypes that are organized into a prompt for code generation. The resulting scoring engine evaluates statistical deviation, structural inconsistency, and density-based abnormality then computes an anomaly score for each test sample. We evaluate LLM-Detector on 24 tabular datasets, comparing against 15 SOTA baselines. Results show consistent improvements across both mixed-type and continuous-only settings. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for LLM fine-tuning or neural network training, reducing computational cost and enabling practical anomaly detection in real-world tabular systems.

</details>


### [166] [When to Retrain: An Empirical Study of Retraining Policies for Streaming ML Under Concept Drift, Budget, and Latency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19488)
*Sawan Dasari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过3,933次受控实验，系统比较了周期性重训练、错误阈值触发和ADWIN统计漂移触发三种模型刷新策略。核心发现是：重训练策略的选择远不如"模型是否增量学习"这一设计决策重要。增量学习下所有策略与不重训练基线无显著差异；非增量学习下策略间准确率差距达15-55个百分点，周期性重训练在突变和渐变漂移下优于两种响应式策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的机器学习系统在概念漂移下性能会退化，但实践者缺乏关于何时重训练的原则性指导。重训练成本高昂、预算有限，且训练和部署延迟会让过期模型在数据持续变化时仍然在线服务。现有研究缺少在统一系统模型下对重训练策略的系统性实证评估。

Method: 在统一系统模型下（显式建模重训练预算和训练加部署延迟），对三种实践中的模型刷新策略（周期性重训练、错误阈值触发、ADWIN统计漂移触发重训练）与不重训练基线进行比较。实验覆盖3,933次运行，涉及三种漂移机制、三种预算水平、多达五种延迟水平、三个数据集和两种学习模式。

Result: 1) 增量学习下，任何策略与不重训练基线在54组配对比较中均无实践上显著的差异，即使在极端延迟下。2) 非增量学习下，策略选择可造成15-55个百分点的漂移后准确率差异，周期性重训练在突变和渐变漂移下显著优于两种响应式策略，响应式策略仅在循环漂移下保有优势。3) 发现了响应式策略的系统性失败模式和延迟-预算队列交互效应，该交互会使有效重训练预算减半。

Conclusion: 重训练策略中最具决定性影响的设计决策不是策略本身（周期vs响应式），而是模型是否采用增量学习。研究揭示了响应式策略的系统性缺陷及延迟与预算间的隐藏交互，并发布了完整模拟器、数据管道和逐次运行工件以供复现。

Abstract: Production machine learning systems degrade under concept drift, yet practitioners have little principled guidance on when to retrain. Retraining is costly, retraining budgets are finite, and a retrained model does not take effect instantly: training and deployment latency leave a stale model serving predictions while the data continues to move. We present a controlled empirical study of three practical model-refresh policies (periodic retraining, error-threshold triggering, and statistical drift-triggered retraining with ADWIN) against a no-retrain baseline, evaluated under a unified system model that makes retraining budgets and training-plus-deployment latency explicit. Across 3,933 experiment runs spanning three drift regimes, three budget levels, up to five latency levels, three datasets, and two learning modes, we find that the single most consequential design decision is not the retraining policy but whether the deployed model learns incrementally. With per-sample incremental updates, and for the linear online learner with immediate labels studied here, no policy differs from the no-retrain baseline by a practically significant margin in any of 54 paired comparisons, even at extreme latency. Without incremental updates, policy choice separates outcomes by 15-55 percentage points of post-drift accuracy, and simple periodic retraining significantly outperforms both reactive policies under abrupt and gradual drift, while reactive policies retain an advantage only under recurring drift. We document systematic failure modes of reactive policies and a latency-budget queueing interaction that silently halves effective retraining budgets, and release the full simulator, dataset pipelines, and per-run artifacts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [167] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeltaMomentum，将动量缓冲器的更新规则从指数移动平均（EMA）改为规范的delta规则，利用线性层梯度的键值结构实现对不同方向的自适应遗忘率。作为任何优化器的即插即用替换，DeltaAdamW在FineWeb-Edu预训练中相比AdamW减少了高达46.39%的训练步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
